11/08/2026, 3:12 am - +44 7912 225796: This message was deleted
11/08/2026, 3:13 am - +44 7912 225796: Pls see this for ease. LOE and package logic added before **Total Client Fee**:

* **Service Fee Field**

  * Each service will have its own **Fee** field, mainly for external/client-facing services.

* **Product & Service Linking**

  * When creating a product, the user will be able to link the product to one or multiple services.

* **Proposal Fee**

  * The fee entered against each service will automatically form part of the **client proposal**.
  * Each service and its respective fee will be clearly shown in the proposal.

* **Separate Fee Columns**

  * Service fees will be displayed as separate columns within the client list/reporting area.
  * For example:

    * VAT Fee
    * Year-End Accounts Fee
    * Payroll Fee
    * Bookkeeping Fee
    * Self Assessment Fee
    * Other Service Fees

* **LOE Signing & Automatic Fee Update**

  * Upon signing the **Letter of Engagement (LOE)**, the agreed fee should automatically update the relevant **Service Fee** field for each service.
  * For example, if the signed LOE includes Year-End Accounts at £1,000 and VAT at £600, these amounts should automatically populate the respective service fee fields.
  * This should ensure that the fee recorded in the client profile always reflects the latest signed engagement.

* **Package Fee**

  * Where a **package** is sold rather than individually priced services, the full package fee should automatically be added to the **Total Fee** section upon signing the LOE.
  * The system should avoid double-counting the package fee and individual service fees.
  * The linked services should still be recorded against the client for workflow, deadline and reporting purposes, even where individual service prices are not available.

* **Total Client Fee**

  * Fees from all individually priced services assigned to a client will automatically be added together.
  * Where a package has been sold, the agreed package price will be used instead.
  * This will populate the **Total Client Fee** field.

* **Accumulated Fees**

  * The total client fee will feed into the **accumulated fee** displayed within the Clients section.

* **Fee Reporting & Analysis**

  * This structure will allow us to track and report:

    * Fee per client, per service
    * Total fee by service
    * Total fee by client
    * Total fee by client type
    * Total package fees
    * Total recurring fees
    * Average fee per client
    * Average fee per service
    * Overall practice fee/revenue value

16/08/2026, 11:21 pm - +44 7912 225796: @⁨Suhaib Osmani⁩ , Small ones. ![generated-image-at-00:00:08](https://loom.com/i/40bfa154c24b4670955620e589c0a42b?workflows_screenshot=true)

## Overview

The Settings page currently mixes active and inactive users in several places, including admin assignments, employee lists, teams, and permissions. This causes removed employees to remain visible and selectable, which can lead to incorrect role assignments and outdated team membership. In addition, email templates do not support checklist attachment display or module-based grouping, making template selection and management harder for users.

## Steps to Reproduce

1. Open **Settings**.
2. Go to the **Admin / Team** section and observe that users who are no longer active still appear in the list.
3. Attempt to remove a user from the team; note whether the user is actually removed from the team and related permissions.
4. Open **Employees** and **Teams** lists and verify that inactive users are still shown instead of being filtered out or marked inactive.
5. Open **Email Templates** and check whether there is an option to display an imported checklist in the template.
6. Create or edit an email template and verify whether templates can be grouped/sorted by module (e.g., Accounts, Clients, Leads, Proposals, Tasks).

## Acceptance Criteria

- Settings pages should display **active users only** by default across admin, employee, team, and permissions views.
- When a user is marked inactive, they should be removed from assigned roles, permissions, and teams automatically.
- Admins should be able to remove a user from a team directly from the Settings page.
- Employee lists should include a visible **role/permission** column.
- Email templates should support showing an imported **checklist** in the template content.
- Email templates should support **module-based grouping/sorting** so relevant templates appear first for the selected module.
- Email popup/template UI should follow a consistent format across all templates.

16/08/2026, 11:22 pm - +44 7912 225796: @⁨~Syed Ali Raza⁩ ![generated-image-at-00:00:13](https://loom.com/i/9e037835bd8c45b8924acdfd686b9a3e?workflows_screenshot=true)

## Overview

The dashboard and several related modules are showing incorrect or incomplete data for employees, tasks, and status summaries. In addition, visibility rules do not appear to be enforced consistently, causing some users to see other employees’ data or partial datasets when they should only see their own, their team’s, or company-wide records based on permissions.

## Steps to Reproduce

1. Open the dashboard as an employee or admin.
2. Review the employee/task summary cards and status sections (e.g. Info Requested, In Progress, Filed, Completed).
3. Observe that some statuses include unrelated items (for example, Info Requested also showing Info Received Reminder) and that counts appear incomplete (e.g. only one item shown in In Progress).
4. Check the overview/sorting behavior for tasks with and without dates.
5. Log in as a non-admin employee and verify whether you can see only your own tasks/data, your team’s data, or all company data depending on the configured permission.
6. Navigate to related modules such as timesheets, capacity planning, employees, services, client summaries, leads, proposals, adherence, and status rules.
7. Confirm whether data loads correctly and whether the same visibility rules are applied consistently across all modules.
8. Review week-based calculations and time summaries to verify the default week range and recorded hours are calculated correctly.

## Acceptance Criteria

- Dashboard should display only active employees and active people/clients where applicable.
- Status cards and task summaries should pull the correct data for each category without mixing unrelated statuses.
- Task overview should sort items correctly, with dated items ordered appropriately and undated items placed as expected.
- Completed date should be shown in tasks and reflected in the dashboard/overview where relevant.
- Permission-based visibility must be enforced consistently across all modules:
  - Own data only
  - Team data
  - Entire company data
- Employees without admin access must not be able to see other employees’ data unless explicitly permitted.
- Timesheets, capacity planning, employees, services, client summaries, leads, proposals, adherence, and status rules should all follow the same visibility model.
- Status rules should use progress statuses consistently and not mix data from different sources.
- Week calculations should default to Monday–Friday, with Saturday/Sunday included only when explicitly configured.
- Data loading errors such as "cannot load the data" should be resolved so the affected views render successfully.

### Overview

17/08/2026, 1:24 am - +44 7912 225796: @⁨Suhaib Osmani⁩ https://www.loom.com/share/8c0fe9e4e6754d699022959c1db7f91d ## Client Status Date Tracking and Bulk Status Action SOP

### Objective

Ensure every client status change is recorded with a clear date and that team members can efficiently bulk unarchive or unfreeze clients using the correct action labels. This SOP helps maintain accurate reporting for archived, frozen, draft, and active clients.

### Key Steps

 

**1. Add a date field for new client creation** [0:00](https://loom.com/share/8c0fe9e4e6754d699022959c1db7f91d?t=0)

![generated-image-at-00:00:00](https://loom.com/i/30fa0f0ede1f4702bd5187009e76cdb7?workflows_screenshot=true)

- When a new client is added, confirm that the **date of creation** is automatically recorded in the client record.
- If the creation date is not already visible, add it to the client view as a dedicated column.
- Verify that the creation date is easy to locate for future reference and reporting.

 

**2. Record the date whenever a client status changes** [0:13](https://loom.com/share/8c0fe9e4e6754d699022959c1db7f91d?t=13)

![generated-image-at-00:00:13](https://loom.com/i/98c5519ce82241b7986ab48ce830761a?workflows_screenshot=true)

- When a client is marked as **archived, frozen, or draft**, ensure the system records the date of that status change.
- Display this date in a visible column so team members can quickly see when the status was applied.
- Use the same date-tracking approach for all relevant client statuses to keep records consistent.

 

**3. Use one activity date column for all status updates** [0:22](https://loom.com/share/8c0fe9e4e6754d699022959c1db7f91d?t=22)

![generated-image-at-00:00:22](https://loom.com/i/34a661e2480a45409a2cef1ae64ddaa3?workflows_screenshot=true)

- Create a single column labeled something like **Activity Date** or **Status Date**.
- Populate this column based on the client’s current status, such as **active, frozen, archived, or draft**.
- Make sure the column reflects the date the client was last marked with that status.
- This allows the team to identify when a client was moved into a specific status without checking multiple fields.

 

**4. Sort and filter clients by status date for reporting** [0:44](https://loom.com/share/8c0fe9e4e6754d699022959c1db7f91d?t=44)

![generated-image-at-00:00:44](https://loom.com/i/a2bd45bb49d54c78b45c769fd55f6dcf?workflows_screenshot=true)

- Use the activity/status date column to sort clients by when they were marked **archived** or **frozen**.
- Filter by month to identify how many clients were archived or frozen during a specific period.
- Confirm that the system can show whether a client was moved to frozen or archived **recently or in the past**.
- Use this data for monthly reporting and status trend analysis.

 

**5. Support bulk unarchive and bulk unfreeze actions** [1:03](https://loom.com/share/8c0fe9e4e6754d699022959c1db7f91d?t=63)

- Ensure the system allows users to perform **bulk unarchive** and **bulk unfreeze** actions.
- Select multiple clients at once and apply the appropriate status reversal in one step.
- Confirm that bulk actions work correctly for both archived and frozen client groups.
- Test that the system updates the client status immediately after the bulk action is completed.

 

**6. Update action labels to match the current client status** [1:16](https://loom.com/share/8c0fe9e4e6754d699022959c1db7f91d?t=76)

![generated-image-at-00:01:16](https://loom.com/i/5e85d201b34b4772a697fc95c45d0f28?workflows_screenshot=true)

- When clients are in the **archived** state, the action should display as **Unarchive**.
- When clients are in the **frozen** state, the action should display as **Unfreeze these clients**.
- Make sure the action label clearly tells the user what will happen next.
- Keep the wording consistent so users do not confuse status changes with other actions.

### Cautionary Notes

- Do not overwrite the original creation date when a client status changes; creation date and activity date should remain distinct if both are used.
- Ensure the activity date always reflects the most recent relevant status change.
- Verify bulk actions only apply to the intended client group to avoid accidental unarchiving or unfreezing.
- Action labels should match the actual status transition exactly to prevent user confusion.

### Tips for Efficiency

- Use a single standardized date field for status changes to simplify filtering and reporting.
- Add sorting and filtering controls for the activity date column to speed up monthly reviews.
- Group archived and frozen clients before applying bulk actions to reduce manual work.
- Keep action labels dynamic so users can immediately understand the available next step.

## Overview

Users are unable to attach a document to a task because the attachment action fails and appears to clear existing task content, including assignees/comments. In addition, overdue company/status information is not displayed consistently in tasks: confirmation statements and accounts show only a red indicator without the company name or overdue/status text, unlike the client view.

## Steps to Reproduce

1. Open an existing task and attempt to attach a document.
2. Observe the attachment failure message and check whether any existing task data (e.g. assignees/comments/attachments) is removed.
3. Open a task for a client with an overdue confirmation statement or accounts item.
4. Compare the task display with the client view.
5. Verify whether the company name, document type (e.g. confirmation statement), and overdue/status text (e.g. "Overdue", "active proposal to strike off") are shown in the task list/details.

## Acceptance Criteria

- Attaching a document to a task succeeds without deleting or clearing existing task data.
- If attachment fails, no existing assignees, comments, or previously added attachments are removed.
- Overdue items in tasks display the company name and relevant document/status text, matching the client view.
- Confirmation statements and accounts show clear overdue/status labels in both client and task contexts.
- The task UI should not rely on color alone; it must include readable status text.

28/08/2026, 8:03 pm - +44 7912 225796: The order to do it in
Data first. Fixing the code before the data is clean just produces a well-engineered wrong answer.

This week, you: turn off the Team filter, get every fee-earner into a team, and confirm working patterns are correct on all 54 active records. Nothing here needs a developer.
This week, devs: make Estimated Time and Internal Deadline required on Custom Task, and pre-fill the estimate from the service template. This stops the leak getting worse while the rest is built.
Next sprint, devs: CP-01 and CP-02 together — change the query from deadline-based to open-during-window, with overdue carried forward. This is the fix that makes the screen usable.
Same sprint, devs: CP-03 — one query behind tiles and grid — and CP-08's caption fixes. Cheap, and they restore trust in the numbers.
Then, devs: CP-04 — real available hours from working patterns, plus the holiday calendar. Bigger, and worth doing properly since it is a selling point for the product.
Alongside, you: the service-template estimate review. It is the slowest item and the one only the practice can do, so start it now rather than when the code is ready.

09/10/2026, 6:42 pm - +44 7912 225796: ### CRM Issues and Bugs to Fix

\- Turnover Pricing Showing £0: The correct price (£168.63) appears in the product picker, but the pricing table and the client’s Letter of Engagement (LOE) show £0.

\- Signature Box Overlapping Fees Table: When the services breakdown is long, the signature and date fields overlap the fees table.

\- Proposal Name Cannot Be Edited: The proposal name is locked to the template name, resulting in every proposal being called “LOE”.

\- Proposal Search Not Working Properly: The search box above the table only searches by proposal name. Searching by lead name returns no results, although the main search bar at the top of the page works.

\- “Is Not” Filter Not Working: Filtering by “Lead name is not TEST” still displays the lead named TEST.

\- Limited Pricing Filters: The pricing filter only offers “is” and “is not”. Greater than and less than options are missing.

\- Automation Email Recipient Restrictions: Automations cannot send follow-up emails directly to leads or clients. The “Send Email” action only allows internal staff as recipients.

\- Incorrect Default BCC: The BCC field automatically populates with the lead’s own email address.

\- Document Builder Freezing: Clicking “Save Proposal” leaves the system stuck on “Generating your PDF…” for over a minute.

\- Preview Opens Live Signing Link: Clicking “Open Preview” opens the actual client signing link instead of a separate preview. Although the page displays “PREVIEW”, it is still the live client link.

\- Hidden PDF Content Visible: Off-screen PDF content, including the firm’s footer address, appears at the bottom of the send-email step.

\- Incorrect Package Billing Period: The package preview displays “/ Year” even when “Monthly” is selected.

\- Package Feature Selection Issue: Ticking the top checkbox in a package column automatically selects or deselects every feature in that column without any warning.

\- Bulk Actions Bar Overlapping Tabs: The bulk-actions bar covers the status tabs, affecting navigation.

\- Missing Lead Names in Kanban: Cards in the “Sent” column do not display the lead’s name.

### Spelling Mistakes and Data Corrections

\- Services:

- “Confirmation Statemnet” → “Confirmation Statement”
- “Persoanl Tax Return” → “Personal Tax Return”

\- Template:

- “Letter of Engagment” → “Letter of Engagement”

\- Pricing Range:

- “Tunrover” → “Turnover”

\- Pricing Variable:

- “Info Recieved” → “Info Received”

\- Services Needed:

remindoo_speed_plan
15/10/2026, 9:20 am - +44 7912 225796: The frontend review is done, and I'd give it 6 out of 10. The core plumbing is solid, but the app is slow mainly because each page downloads far too much JavaScript. The server itself responds quickly.
Huge page downloads. The Clients page pulls in about 1.4 MB of compressed JavaScript, and Tasks and Dashboard about 0.8 to 1 MB each. That's because the Excel import, PDF viewer, charts and text editors are loaded upfront even when the page doesn't show them. Loading them only when they're needed is the biggest quick win.
The app shell loads everywhere. Even the login page loads the sidebar, wallet and timer code, and calls the AI-provider and balance endpoints.
Requests that wait on each other. Tasks waits for the assignees, then the saved filters, and only then loads the tasks. The dashboard does something similar.
Backend slowness on top. Each API call costs about 0.3 to 1 second, which I covered in the backend review.
Fixing the first and fourth together would make the biggest noticeable difference.
15/10/2026, 9:20 am - +44 7912 225796: The backend review is done. I'd give it 5.5 out of 10. The core logic is sensible, but the main files are huge: the clients service alone is about 10,000 lines, which makes changes risky. A good share of the slowness comes from the backend:
Session check on every request. Each call looks up the session in Upstash first, which adds about 300 ms. The dashboard makes eight calls, so it takes about 2.5 seconds even with no data.
Profile and login. Profile takes about 0.8 seconds because of an extra Supabase lookup, and login takes 3.3 seconds.
Task list ignores page size. It always returns 100 rows (or "all") whatever the page asks for, and takes about 1 second even when empty.
Dashboard task summary. It runs about eight queries per load and takes 1 to 3.8 seconds.
Client lists. Each page triggers about 18 extra queries, and invoice filters load up to 10,000 rows into memory.
Those times are from an empty test account, so with real data they'll be worse. I'm still waiting on the frontend review, and then I'll send the combined mind map.

26/10/2026, 12:41 am - +44 7912 225796: : Remindoo – postcode lookup for addresses not coming from Companies House

Team,

Rule: only add the postcodes.io lookup (https://postcodes.io/) where the address is NOT already coming from Companies House. If CH provides the address, we rely on CH and nothing changes.

Already covered, so don’t change these:

Company registered office: from Companies House
Director and officer addresses: from Companies House
Billing address: from Stripe
AML / ID&V address: from our third-party AML provider

Action: please audit every address field in Remindoo and list any that are not sourced from CH, Stripe or the AML provider. Add the postcode lookup to those. Likely candidates:

Sole traders / self-employed: home and business address
Landlords: home address, plus a separate address for each rental property
Individuals, partnerships, unincorporated charities and trusts
Leads Pipeline: lead capture and web forms (before the CH link exists)
Proposals and LOE / E-Signature: where the client isn’t a company
Client Portal: onboarding forms where clients enter their own details
Staff / employee onboarding: HR address
CSV client import: clean non-CH addresses using bulk POST /postcodes

How it works

As the user types, call GET /postcodes/{partial}/autocomplete.
When a postcode is selected, call GET /postcodes/{postcode} to auto-fill Town, County and Country.
The user types Address line 1 and line 2, because postcodes.io doesn’t return street or house numbers.
Block invalid postcodes. Format postcodes in uppercase with the standard space.
Overseas addresses: manual entry with a country dropdown.
Store the address source on each record: CH, Stripe, AML provider, postcode lookup or manual.

29/10/2026, 6:21 pm - +44 7912 225796: @⁨Suhaib Osmani⁩ @⁨Umair Saeed⁩ @⁨AR - 14⁩  Here are the Remindoo bugs I found while setting up the Limited Company products:

1. Proposal product picker shows raw HTML in descriptions (high)

Where: Proposals → Products and Pricing → “+ Products” → Select Products panel.
What happens: descriptions show raw code like <ul><li>…</li></ul>, &pound;, &ndash; and &amp; instead of formatted bullets.
Expected: the formatted description saved from the editor, with bullets and £ signs.
Fix: display the description as formatted content, cleaned for safety first (e.g. with DOMPurify), and decode the escaped characters. The saved data is already correct.

2. Saving a product removes it from its category (high)

Where: Settings → Proposal Setting → Products → Edit → Update.
What happens: after saving, the product’s category shows N/A and the category’s product count drops. All 21 Limited Company products lost their category this way.
Expected: the category stays as it was unless the user changes it.
Likely cause: the update sends an empty category list, or replaces the existing category link instead of keeping it.

3. Category picked in the product form doesn’t save (high)

Where: the Edit Product form → Choose Categories.
What happens: a category chosen here doesn’t stick after saving. It only sticks when products are added from the Categories tab (category “+” → Products).
Expected: both routes save the category.

4. Edit Product form sometimes opens without its category (medium)

What happens: Choose Categories shows empty even when the product belongs to a category. It’s linked to bug 2: saving from this state wipes the category.

5. Product search doesn’t rerun on the same text (low)

Where: Products tab search.
What happens: after saving a product, the list goes back to all 153 products while the search box still shows the old text. Pressing Enter on that same text does nothing.
Expected: saving keeps the current search results, or searching again on the same text works.

6. “Add products” list in a category only shows the last search (low)

Where: Categories → category “+” → Products → Available Products.
What happens: it only lists products that matched the last search on the Products tab, and its own search box finds nothing outside that list.
Expected: it searches all products.

7. Package Preview period mismatch (low)

Where: Packages → Group → Preview (eye icon).
What happens: the dropdown says “Monthly” but each card shows “£199 / Year”. The £199 is the monthly price; the yearly price is £2,388.
Expected: the label and price match the selected period.

30/10/2026, 4:49 pm - +44 7912 225796: Remindoo CRM – Notes must appear on the Lead Timeline

Problem
Notes added on a lead (from the Notes section in the lead panel, a task, a call log or anywhere else) don’t appear in the Lead Timeline. At the moment the timeline only shows “Lead Added”, so it doesn’t give a full history of the lead.

Requirement
Every note added to a lead must also show as an entry in Lead → Timeline, wherever the note was added from.

Acceptance criteria

Notes from every entry point show on the timeline as “Note Added”: the lead side panel Notes section, the timeline’s own “+ Add notes” button, notes on tasks, call logs, proposals and emails, and notes created by import or the API.
Each entry shows the full note text, the author’s name and avatar, and the date and time (UK format, Europe/London). Notes are grouped by day, like the existing “TODAY” header.
If a note is edited, the timeline entry updates and shows “Edited [date/time] by [user]”. If a note is deleted, the entry stays as “Note deleted by [user]” for the audit trail.
Notes can be found with the “Search timeline…” box. The Filter icon gets a Notes type, and the “Latest First / Oldest First” sort applies to notes.
Existing notes are backfilled into the timeline with their original timestamps.
When a lead is converted to a client (the “Convert to Client” button), the full timeline, including notes, carries over to the client record.
Notes added from the side panel show on the timeline straight away, without a page refresh.

Suggested build
Write a timeline event (note_created, note_updated, note_deleted) inside the single notes service, so every entry point is covered and none can be missed. Don’t build this per screen.

Priority: High. Accountants rely on lead history for AML and onboarding evidence, and for handovers within the team.
